# Zodiac bot: results

Generated from `results/ledger.jsonl`. Do not edit by hand.

Hypotheses run: 2159 (excluding calibration: 2153).

How to read this: **p** compares the real cipher with shuffled copies of its own symbols. **power** is how often genuine English, enciphered the same way, beats the nulls. If power is low, a low or high p means nothing. **p (Holm)** corrects within the row's tier. Exploration is screening only and never alerts: a low exploratory p should be re-run as a queued (confirmatory) test.
